# ComputerPets — Implementation Roadmap

This document outlines the phased plan to evolve the EnterprisePet Backend from its current secure baseline into a production-ready, scalable service.

> **Current Status (May 2026):** All P0 security items + **Phase 1 Production Readiness Foundations** have been completed.
> **Phase 2** (Security & Reliability Hardening) is complete. Current focus: close remaining Phase 3 observability gaps and other non-storefront, non-DX12 release-readiness work. See [ARCHITECTURE.md](ARCHITECTURE.md) §10.

---

## Phase 0: Security & Quality Baseline (Completed)

All critical items required before any public or limited production exposure have been delivered:

- Real Steam Web API integration + provider enable/disable toggles
- Proper ABI decoding for NFT ownership verification
- Default `LICENSE_SECRET_KEY` now fails hard outside of tests
- Unit tests for all three ownership providers

**Outcome:** The service is now safe for internal development and limited testing.

---

## Phase 1: Production Readiness Foundations (Completed — May 2026)

**Goal:** Make the service safe, observable, and operationally ready for internal / limited production use.

### 1.1 Observability Baseline
- [x] Add Spring Boot Actuator + Prometheus metrics
- [x] Expose proper health, readiness, and liveness probes (`/actuator/health/liveness`, `/readiness`)
- [x] Add structured logging (request IDs, correlation IDs via MDC + `RequestMdcFilter`)
- [x] Custom health indicators (`SteamHealthIndicator`) + GlobalExceptionHandler with RFC 7807 ProblemDetails

### 1.2 Basic Persistence Layer
- [x] JPA `IssuedLicense` entity with jti, owner, pet, provider, timestamps, `revokedAt`, `deletedAt` (soft-delete; ADR 0058)
- [x] `LicenseRepository` (JpaRepository + `findByJti` + active-only queries)
- [x] Full revocation checks integrated into `LicenseService.validate()` (and issuance persistence)
- [x] Flyway migrations (`V1` issued licenses, `V2` hwid/lastUsed, `V3` soft-delete + `license_audit_events`) + `ddl-auto=validate`

### 1.3 CI/CD & Containerization
- [x] GitHub Actions (`ci.yml`): build/test + separate GHCR publish job (multi-arch tags)
- [x] Multi-stage `Dockerfile` (Eclipse Temurin 21, non-root `appuser`, healthcheck with wget)
- [x] `docker-compose.yml` (app + Postgres 16 + optional WireMock profile) with proper health conditions
- [x] `.dockerignore`, externalized Steam base URL + secrets via env

### 1.4 API Contract & Documentation
- [x] Full springdoc-openapi + rich `@Schema` DTOs (VerifySuccessResponse, DownloadResponse, ErrorResponse, PetInfo, etc.)
- [x] Centralized `ApiExamples` class with 25+ reusable request/response/error examples (all 3 providers, download flows, 10+ error variants)
- [x] Professional docs/ folder (ARCHITECTURE.md living doc, ROADMAP.md, SETUP.md, CONTRIBUTING.md, README index)
- [x] Root README + .github/ issue/PR templates

**Outcome:** The backend is now production-foundation ready. Docker images publish to GHCR, Postgres-backed, fully observable, and have excellent machine-readable API contracts.

**Phase 1 completed May 2026.**

---

## Phase 2: Security & Reliability Hardening (Completed — September 2026)

**Goal:** Significantly improve defense-in-depth and reduce operational risk.

- **2.1 Download Authorization Hardening**
  - [x] jti-bound signed download URLs (signature now `pet|owner|jti|exp`)
  - [x] Usage recording (`lastUsedAt` on IssuedLicense) on every successful download via /api/download
  - [x] One-time-use + IP binding on the jti foundation (`DownloadGrantIndex`; `GET /api/bundles/{pet}/redeem`; deny-safe second use and address mismatch; ADR 0055)
  - [x] Signed machine verify — `POST /api/verify/{provider}` requires HMAC-SHA256 (`X-ComputerPets-Timestamp`, `X-ComputerPets-Signature`, 300s skew, `LICENSE_SECRET_KEY`). Download stays the license JWT. Redeem stays the URL HMAC (ADR 0070). Catalog stays 221.
  - [x] Signed admin requests — every `/api/admin/**` method except OPTIONS requires HMAC-SHA256 (`computerpets-admin-v1`, 300s skew, `ADMIN_API_KEY`). A static `X-Admin-Key` is refused (ADR 0071). Catalog stays 221.
  - [x] Single-use nonce — signed admin and machine requests send `X-ComputerPets-Nonce` (in the MAC). Replay inside 300 seconds is **401**. The nonce store shares Redis with the rate limiter (`replay:nonce:…`, `SET NX EX 300`). Store down is **503** (ADR 0072). Catalog stays 221.
  - [x] Single-use download JWT — `JwtService` mints `jti`. `POST /api/download` claims `download:jwt:{jti}` once (`SET NX`, TTL `jwt.ttl-minutes` + 60s). A second mint is **409**. A bearer with no `jti` is **401**. Store down is **503**. Unlock still sends the bearer; it does not add a jti field (ADR 0073). Catalog stays 221.
  - [x] Regional WAF in front of those buckets — default block, 10/30/60/60 per minute on the API ALB, signed redeem excluded. Plan refuses an empty ALB ARN (ADR 0074). Catalog stays 221.
  - [x] Redis AUTH and transit TLS — optional `REDIS_PASSWORD` / `REDIS_PASSWORD_FILE` and `REDIS_SSL` on the one Lettuce client. Prod fails closed when AUTH is required and the password is missing. ElastiCache enables AUTH and transit encryption together when `redis_auth_token` is set (ADR 0075). Catalog stays 221.
  - [x] Postgres transit TLS — managed JDBC `sslmode=require` (or `verify-full` when a CA path is set) and RDS `rds.force_ssl=1`. Prod refuses a half-set pair. Local and in-cluster Postgres stay cleartext (ADR 0076). Catalog stays 221.
  - [x] API listener TLS — cert-manager Ingress or an ACM HTTPS listener on the keeper-owned API ALB. Port 80 redirects. The JVM stays HTTP on 8081. Prod refuses a cleartext public origin when the flag is set. This root does not call ACM (ADR 0077). Catalog stays 221.
  - [x] API horizontal pod autoscaling — `deploy/k8s/hpa.yaml` targets `computerpets-blue` (min 3, max 3, CPU 70% of the request, memory at 800Mi). Not in the kustomization. Local apply stays blue 2 / green 0. Resource metrics are `metrics-server.yaml` (ADR 0078, ADR 0084, ADR 0108). Catalog stays 221.
  - [x] API pod disruption budget — `deploy/k8s/pdb.yaml` keeps 2 pods of the live color (`color=blue`) during voluntary disruption. Not in the kustomization. Apply only after the HPA floor is running. Cutover patches the selector with the HPA (ADR 0079). Catalog stays 221.
  - [x] API hostname spread — both Deployments set `topologySpreadConstraints` on `kubernetes.io/hostname` (`maxSkew: 1`, `ScheduleAnyway`, `nodeTaintsPolicy: Honor`, selector scoped to that color). Not `DoNotSchedule`. Not a required anti-affinity. Local replica counts stay blue 2 / green 0 (ADR 0080). Catalog stays 221.
  - [x] API zone spread — both Deployments add `topology.kubernetes.io/zone` (`maxSkew: 1`, `ScheduleAnyway`, `nodeTaintsPolicy: Honor`). Not `DoNotSchedule`. Prod expects at least two availability zones. Local replica counts stay blue 2 / green 0 (ADR 0081). Catalog stays 221.
  - [x] Multi-AZ API node pool — one private EKS managed node group per availability zone, at least two, on a keeper-owned cluster. No public IP. No SSH. Plan refuses a single zone. EKS sets the zone label from the instance AZ. This root does not create the cluster (ADR 0082). Catalog stays 221.
  - [x] Cluster Autoscaler — grows those node groups when pods are Pending. Per-zone max is at least the HPA ceiling of 10. Terraform ignores `desired_size` after create. IRSA trusts only `kube-system/cluster-autoscaler`. The manifest is not in the kustomization. Kind/minikube stay off (ADR 0083). Catalog stays 221.
  - [x] metrics-server — `deploy/k8s/metrics-server.yaml` is upstream v0.9.0 so `hpa.yaml` can read CPU and memory. Not in the kustomization. Kubelet TLS stays verified. Apply before the HPA (ADR 0084). Catalog stays 221.
  - [x] metrics-server high availability — same manifest is upstream `high-availability-1.21+.yaml` v0.9.0. `replicas: 2`, required pod anti-affinity on `kubernetes.io/hostname`, addon PDB `minAvailable: 1`. Not in the kustomization. No `--kubelet-insecure-tls` (ADR 0085). Catalog stays 221.
  - [x] metrics-server kubelet CA — `--kubelet-certificate-authority` mounts operator ConfigMap or Secret `metrics-server-kubelet-ca` (`ca.crt`, `optional: false`). No `--kubelet-insecure-tls`. The CA bytes are not in git. Not in the kustomization (ADR 0086). Catalog stays 221.
  - [x] metrics-server serving certificate — `--tls-cert-file` and `--tls-private-key-file` mount Secret `metrics-server-serving`. `insecureSkipTLSVerify` is unset. The cert bytes are not in git. A private CA is keeper-set `caBundle`. Not in the kustomization (ADR 0087). Catalog stays 221.
  - [x] metrics-server zone spread — `topologySpreadConstraints` on `topology.kubernetes.io/zone` (`maxSkew: 1`, `ScheduleAnyway`, `nodeTaintsPolicy: Honor`) beside required hostname anti-affinity. Not `DoNotSchedule`. A single-zone cluster still schedules. Not in the kustomization (ADR 0088). Catalog stays 221.
  - [x] metrics-server node pool pin — `nodeSelector` requires `kubernetes.io/os: linux` and `computerpets/node-pool: api`. `nodeAffinityPolicy: Honor` keeps the zone count on those nodes. Kind and minikube do not apply the file. A single-zone set of labeled nodes still schedules. Not in the kustomization (ADR 0089). Catalog stays 221.
  - [x] Cluster Autoscaler high availability — `replicas: 2`, required hostname anti-affinity, preferred zone anti-affinity, leader election on the leases lock. Rolling update does not ask for a third hostname. Same IRSA service account. Not in the kustomization (ADR 0090). Catalog stays 221.
  - [x] Cluster Autoscaler node-pool pin — `nodeSelector` requires `kubernetes.io/os: linux` and `computerpets/node-pool: api`. Required hostname anti-affinity stays inside that pool. Kind and minikube do not apply the file. A single-zone set of labeled nodes still schedules. The API pin is ADR 0093. Not in the kustomization (ADR 0091). Catalog stays 221.
  - [x] Cluster Autoscaler disruption budget — `policy/v1` `PodDisruptionBudget` in `cluster-autoscaler.yaml`, `minAvailable: 1`, selector `app=cluster-autoscaler`. One pod can drain while the other holds or takes the lease. `minAvailable: 2` is not used. Kind and minikube do not apply the file. Not in the kustomization (ADR 0092). Catalog stays 221.
  - [x] API node-pool pin — blue and green `nodeSelector` requires `kubernetes.io/os: linux` and `computerpets/node-pool: api`. `nodeAffinityPolicy: Honor` keeps hostname and zone spread on those nodes. Kind and minikube leave the API pods Pending until a node carries the label. One labeled node still schedules. HPA and PDB stay (ADR 0093). Catalog stays 221.
  - [x] API pool taint — the node group taints `computerpets/node-pool=api:NoSchedule`. Blue, green, metrics-server, and Cluster Autoscaler tolerate it. Postgres and Redis do not. Kind and minikube are not tainted (ADR 0094). Catalog stays 221.
  - [x] API zone hard spread — both colors set `topology.kubernetes.io/zone` to `DoNotSchedule` (`maxSkew` 1, `nodeAffinityPolicy: Honor`). One labeled zone still schedules. A node that omits the zone label does not. Hostname stays `ScheduleAnyway`. HPA and PDB stay (ADR 0095). Catalog stays 221.
  - [x] API pool system DaemonSet toleration — `vpc-cni` `configuration_values` records `operator: Exists` and `computerpets/node-pool=api:NoSchedule` (`Equal`) for `aws-node`. `kube-proxy` records the `Equal` entry in a strategic-merge patch. The addon schema rejects `tolerations`. Kind and minikube stay untainted (ADR 0096). Catalog stays 221.
  - [x] kube-proxy toleration hook — `reassert_kube_proxy_toleration=true` probes on plan and reasserts coverage on apply, or fails closed. The flag defaults false. Kind and minikube are refused before any patch (ADR 0097). Catalog stays 221.
  - [x] metrics-server zone hard spread — `DoNotSchedule` on `topology.kubernetes.io/zone` (`maxSkew` 1, Honor policies). One labeled zone still schedules. A node that omits the zone label does not. Replicas stay 2. Kind and minikube do not apply the file (ADR 0098). Catalog stays 221.
  - [x] Cluster Autoscaler zone hard spread — `DoNotSchedule` on `topology.kubernetes.io/zone` (`maxSkew` 1, Honor policies). The preferred zone term is removed. One labeled zone still schedules. A node that omits the zone label does not. Replicas stay 2. Kind and minikube do not apply the file (ADR 0099). Catalog stays 221.
  - [x] Cluster Autoscaler leader scale path — the standby is not the scaler. The scheduled leader keeps `--balance-similar-node-groups=true`, `--expander=least-waste`, and `--skip-nodes-with-system-pods=false`. One similar group per zone, at least two. Kind and minikube do not apply the file (ADR 0101). Catalog stays 221.
  - [x] Cluster Autoscaler scale-up salvo — `--salvo-scale-up=true` and `--salvo-scale-up-budget=1m` run another least-waste choice in the same loop. `--frequent-loops-enabled=true` stays. No priority expander ConfigMap. Kind and minikube do not apply the file (ADR 0102). Catalog stays 221.
  - [x] Cluster Autoscaler salvo early stop — the `1m` budget stays. Node-provision time and node-group backoff stay at the v1.36.1 defaults. A budget that is already gone, a failed snapshot update, or a scale-up that is not successful still ends the salvo. The next main loop is the retry. Kind and minikube do not apply the file (ADR 0103). Catalog stays 221.
  - [x] Per-zone API node-group max — `max_size` is 20. The HPA ceiling is now 3 (ADR 0108). Cluster Autoscaler reads the Auto Scaling group `MaxSize` and cannot lift it. The old max of 10 is refused. The per-zone min of 1 is ADR 0105. Kind and minikube do not apply the file (ADR 0104). Catalog stays 221.
  - [x] API hostname floor — each zone's `min_size` is 2 and the create-time `desired_size` is 2. Two healthy zones are four hostnames, so the HPA floor of 3 does not land 2 and 1. HPA min stays 3 because the PDB keeps 2. The old floor of 1 is refused. Kind and minikube do not plan the pool. Do not set minDomains (ADR 0105). Catalog stays 221.
  - [x] Single-zone API hostname floor — each zone's `min_size` is 3 and the create-time `desired_size` is 3. One Ready zone is three hostnames, so the HPA floor of 3 is 1 and 1 and 1. The zone gate stays two. The floor of 2 is refused. Kind and minikube do not plan the pool. Do not set minDomains (ADR 0106). Catalog stays 221.
  - [x] API hostname ceiling — `maxReplicas` is 6, two zones times `min_size` 3. A healthy pool holds that ceiling at one pod per hostname. The old ceiling of 10 is refused. `min_size` stays 3. `max_size` stays 20. Kind and minikube do not apply the HPA. Do not set minDomains (ADR 0107). Catalog stays 221.
  - [x] Single-zone API hostname ceiling — `maxReplicas` is 3, equal to the HPA floor and to `min_size` 3. One Ready zone holds that ceiling at one pod per hostname. The ceiling of 6 is refused. Eight, ten, twelve, and twenty node bills are refused. Kind and minikube do not apply the HPA. Do not set minDomains (ADR 0108). Catalog stays 221.
  - [x] Per-zone max floor — `max_size` stays 20, above a one-zone floor of 8 (HPA ceiling 3 + Cluster Autoscaler 2 + metrics-server 2 + one drain node). Twice the new ceiling is 6 and is refused. The live set binds on `min_size` 3, so MaxLimitReached at 20 is unreachable. Kind and minikube do not apply the file. Do not set minDomains (ADR 0109). Catalog stays 221.
  - [x] Pending Cluster Autoscaler lease — a Pending pod never holds `leases/cluster-autoscaler`. v1.36.1 acquires it inside the running container. The Running leader remains the scaler. `priorityClassName` stays `system-cluster-critical`. Kind and minikube do not apply the file. Do not set minDomains (ADR 0110). Catalog stays 221.
  - [x] metrics-server serving chain — the leaf DNS SAN is `metrics-server.kube-system.svc` and it chains to the private CA written into APIService `caBundle`. Apply refuses a bad chain or a bad SAN before kubectl. Kind and minikube do not apply the file. No `--kubelet-insecure-tls` (ADR 0111). Catalog stays 221.

- **2.2 Hardware Binding (hwid)**
  - [x] Optional `hwid` stored on IssuedLicense + inside the encrypted LicensePayload
  - [x] Enforced at download time when the license was originally issued with a device binding

- **2.3 Resilience Patterns**
  - [x] Circuit breakers + retries (Resilience4j) added for Steam, Microsoft, NFT, Itch, and Epic providers (with per-provider configuration in application.yml)
  - [x] Timeouts and graceful degradation (fallbacks deny safely)
    - [x] Overlay IPC news, quote, and radio reads time out at twelve seconds and return unread with an empty plate. The keeper line stays can't reach. A radio timeout does not call the next directory host. Weather forecast and geocode page wrappers time out the same way and flip unread / can't reach. Desk and overlay news, quote, and radio page wrappers (RSS, featured Wikipedia, CoinGecko, GeckoTerminal, Yahoo, Radio Find) time out the same way. Cloud talk and cloud voice page wrappers time out the same way and keep the house line or silence. Steam, Itch, and Epic RestClients time out at ten seconds and deny. Microsoft and NFT already time out. Shared Resilience4j ownership time limiter (`ownership`, twelve-second wall) wraps Steam / Itch / Epic / Microsoft probes; exceed denies. RestClient still owns the per-HTTP ten-second hop. Not a new host. Not invented weather or entitlements. Catalog stays 221.
  - [x] Microsoft Store verify uses Collections v9 `publisherQuery`; prod still refuses dev-mode; live Store ID is still a publish-time config, not invented here.

- **2.4 Secret Management**
  - [x] All critical secrets use the same strict fail-hard placeholder pattern (`LICENSE_SECRET_KEY`, `JWT_*`, `BUNDLE_*`, `ADMIN_API_KEY`)
  - [x] docker-compose and application.yml carry the admin key
  - [x] Production path: Docker `*_FILE` mounts + Kubernetes External Secrets / Vault agent templates into the existing Opaque Secret (ADR 0056, `SecretFileEnvironmentPostProcessor`, `docker-compose.secrets.yml`, `deploy/k8s/external-secret.example.yaml`). Local-dev keeps env / `.env.example`. Deny-safe: missing file → refuse start; missing optional storefront key → refuse the feature; never invent a production secret; never log secret values. Not a hosted Vault deploy.
  - [x] Prod refuse plain env Secret — `COMPUTERPETS_SECRETS_SOURCE` + `verify-secret-operator.sh` (ADR 0064).
  - [x] Secret rotation cadence + dual-key verify / decrypt (`*_PREVIOUS`, optional `COMPUTERPETS_KEYS_ROTATED_AT`, HSM/KMS pointer; ADR 0065).

- **Admin Tooling (new)**
  - [x] `POST /api/admin/revoke` — revokes any license by jti using the existing persistence layer. Protected by the admin request HMAC (ADR 0071).

- **2.5 NFT entitlement hardening (Aug 2026)**
  - [x] Reject malformed wallets (`0x`, short hex) instead of substring-matching ABI words
  - [x] Official collection allowlist (`ethereum.collections`) so a random ERC-721 cannot mint a pet license
  - [x] Optional `tokenId → petType` binding; mismatch is a 403
  - [x] ERC-1155 `balanceOf` + `AUTO` fallback
  - [x] Required `personal_sign` proof of wallet control (fail closed)
  - [x] RPC timeouts, placeholder-RPC health indicator, `GET /api/verify/nft/collections`

## Phase 3: Scalability & Operational Maturity

**Goal:** Prepare the service for horizontal scaling and production traffic.

- **3.1 Distributed State**
  - [x] Redis-backed rate limiting (replace in-memory Bucket4j)
  - [x] Distributed revocation / jti blacklist
  - [x] Bundle catalog reads (`GET /api/bundles/{petKey}`) 60/min fail-closed; signed redeem excluded (ADR 0069)

- **3.2 Observability Maturity**
  - [x] Distributed tracing (Micrometer + OpenTelemetry)
  - [x] Custom business metrics (verification success rate, latency per provider)
  - [x] License issuance rate (`enterprisepet.license.issue` on verify→issue; provider + pet + outcome; ADR 0057)

- **3.2b Database & Persistence Maturity**
  - [x] Soft deletion + audit logging for licenses (`deletedAt` on revoke; `license_audit_events` ISSUED / REVOKED / DOWNLOAD; ADR 0058)
  - [x] Hikari pool defaults + optional deny-safe read replica (`SPRING_DATASOURCE_REPLICA_URL`; ADR 0059)
  - [x] Postgres transit TLS — `sslmode=require` or `verify-full` on the managed JDBC URL; `rds.force_ssl=1`; prod fails closed when half-set (ADR 0076)

- **3.3 Deployment & Environments**
  - [x] Proper Spring profiles (`dev` / `staging` / `prod`)
  - [x] Kubernetes manifests (`deploy/k8s/`, not Helm)
  - [x] Blue/green via two Deployments + Service `color` selector (no mesh)
  - [x] GHCR image signing — keyless cosign (Sigstore Fulcio) on `main` publish; fail-closed digest verify for prod deploy (`deploy/k8s/verify-image-signature.sh`; ADR 0061)
  - [x] Terraform for managed stores — Postgres / Redis / Secrets Manager shells / CDN / WAF (`deploy/terraform/`; deny-safe defaults; ADR 0062)
  - [x] WAF live gate — regional ACL matches the JVM rate-limit buckets; `terraform plan` refuses to apply without the API ALB ARN (ADR 0074)
  - [x] CDN edge redeem verification — fail-closed call to house `GET /api/bundles/{pet}/redeem` before zip bytes (`deploy/cdn/edge-redeem.js`; `pet=` on signed URLs; ADR 0063)
  - [x] Secret-operator hardening — prod refuses plain env / hand-filled Opaque Secret without `COMPUTERPETS_SECRETS_SOURCE` ∈ {`external-secrets`, `file`, `vault-agent`}; `verify-secret-operator.sh` deploy gate (ADR 0064). Local-dev keeps env / scaffolding `secret.yaml`.
  - [x] Secret rotation cadence / HSM story — dual-key `*_PREVIOUS` verify/decrypt, documented 90d/180d cadence, optional `COMPUTERPETS_KEYS_ROTATED_AT` (400d max when set), KMS/HSM pointer without a live appliance (`verify-secret-rotation.sh`; ADR 0065).
  - [x] API listener TLS — public door is `ingress-tls.yaml` or an ALB HTTPS listener (port 80 redirects). JVM stays HTTP on 8081. Prod refuses a cleartext public origin when `API_LISTENER_TLS_REQUIRED` is set. This root does not call ACM (ADR 0077). Catalog stays 221.
  - [x] API horizontal pod autoscaling — prod `hpa.yaml` (min 3, max 3) is not in the kustomization. Local apply stays unscaled. Resource metrics are the metrics-server manifest (ADR 0078, ADR 0084, ADR 0108). Catalog stays 221.
  - [x] API pod disruption budget — prod `pdb.yaml` (`minAvailable: 2` on the live color) is not in the kustomization. Apply only after the HPA floor is running (ADR 0079). Catalog stays 221.
  - [x] API hostname spread — soft `topologySpreadConstraints` on `kubernetes.io/hostname` for both colors (`ScheduleAnyway`). A one-node local apply still schedules (ADR 0080). Catalog stays 221.
  - [x] API zone spread — soft `topologySpreadConstraints` on `topology.kubernetes.io/zone` for both colors (`ScheduleAnyway`). A single-zone local apply still schedules. Prod expects at least two availability zones (ADR 0081). Catalog stays 221.
  - [x] Multi-AZ API node pool — private workers, one managed node group per AZ, at least two. No public IP. No SSH. The cluster stays the keeper's (ADR 0082). Catalog stays 221.
  - [x] Cluster Autoscaler — scales those groups when pods are Pending. Max per zone is at least the HPA ceiling. Terraform ignores desired size after create. Not in the kustomization (ADR 0083). Catalog stays 221.
  - [x] metrics-server — upstream v0.9.0 manifest, not in the kustomization. HPA resource metrics stay dark until `kubectl top` answers. Kubelet TLS stays verified (ADR 0084). Catalog stays 221.
  - [x] metrics-server high availability — `replicas: 2` and required hostname anti-affinity, plus an addon PDB that keeps one pod. Still not in the kustomization. No kubelet TLS skip (ADR 0085). Catalog stays 221.
  - [x] metrics-server kubelet CA — operator ConfigMap or Secret `metrics-server-kubelet-ca` mounted read-only for `--kubelet-certificate-authority`. No kubelet TLS skip. Still not in the kustomization (ADR 0086). Catalog stays 221.
  - [x] metrics-server serving certificate — Secret `metrics-server-serving` mounted read-only for `--tls-cert-file` and `--tls-private-key-file`. `insecureSkipTLSVerify` stays off. Still not in the kustomization (ADR 0087). Catalog stays 221.
  - [x] metrics-server zone spread — soft zone spread beside required hostname anti-affinity (`ScheduleAnyway` on `topology.kubernetes.io/zone`). A single-zone apply still schedules. Still not in the kustomization (ADR 0088). Catalog stays 221.
  - [x] metrics-server node pool pin — required `nodeSelector` on `computerpets/node-pool=api` with `nodeAffinityPolicy: Honor`. Kind stays off the file. A labeled single-zone cluster still schedules. Still not in the kustomization (ADR 0089). Catalog stays 221.

  - [x] API pool taint — `computerpets/node-pool=api:NoSchedule` on the API node group, with matching tolerations on the four workloads that already select it. Kind and minikube stay untainted (ADR 0094). Catalog stays 221.
  - [x] API zone hard spread — `DoNotSchedule` on `topology.kubernetes.io/zone` for blue and green (`maxSkew` 1). One labeled zone still schedules. Hostname stays `ScheduleAnyway` (ADR 0095). Catalog stays 221.
  - [x] API pool system DaemonSet toleration — `aws-node` via `vpc-cni` `configuration_values`, `kube-proxy` via a strategic-merge patch. Kind and minikube stay untainted (ADR 0096). Catalog stays 221.
  - [x] kube-proxy toleration hook — probe on plan and reassert on apply, or fail closed. The live flag defaults false. Kind and minikube stay untainted (ADR 0097). Catalog stays 221.
  - [x] metrics-server zone hard spread — `DoNotSchedule` on `topology.kubernetes.io/zone` (`maxSkew` 1). One labeled zone still schedules. Replicas stay 2. Kind and minikube do not apply the file (ADR 0098). Catalog stays 221.
  - [x] Cluster Autoscaler zone hard spread — `DoNotSchedule` on `topology.kubernetes.io/zone` (`maxSkew` 1, Honor policies). The preferred zone term is removed. One labeled zone still schedules. A node that omits the zone label does not. Replicas stay 2. Kind and minikube do not apply the file (ADR 0099). Catalog stays 221.
  - [x] Cluster Autoscaler leader scale path — the standby is not the scaler. The scheduled leader keeps `--balance-similar-node-groups=true` and `--expander=least-waste`. One similar group per zone. Kind and minikube do not apply the file (ADR 0101). Catalog stays 221.
  - [x] Cluster Autoscaler scale-up salvo — `--salvo-scale-up=true` and `--salvo-scale-up-budget=1m` run another least-waste choice in the same loop. `--frequent-loops-enabled=true` stays. No priority expander ConfigMap. Kind and minikube do not apply the file (ADR 0102). Catalog stays 221.
  - [x] Cluster Autoscaler salvo early stop — the `1m` budget stays. Node-provision time and node-group backoff stay at the v1.36.1 defaults. Kind and minikube do not apply the file (ADR 0103). Catalog stays 221.
  - [x] Per-zone API node-group max — `max_size` is 20. The HPA ceiling is now 3 (ADR 0108). Cluster Autoscaler reads the Auto Scaling group `MaxSize` and cannot lift it.   - [x] API hostname floor — each zone's `min_size` is 2 and the create-time `desired_size` is 2. Two healthy zones are four hostnames, so the HPA floor of 3 does not land 2 and 1.   - [x] Single-zone API hostname floor — each zone's `min_size` is 3 and the create-time `desired_size` is 3. One Ready zone is three hostnames, so the HPA floor of 3 is 1 and 1 and 1. The zone gate stays two. The floor of 2 is refused. Kind and minikube do not plan the pool. Do not set minDomains (ADR 0106). Catalog stays 221.
  - [x] API hostname ceiling — `maxReplicas` is 6, two zones times `min_size` 3. A healthy pool holds that ceiling at one pod per hostname. The old ceiling of 10 is refused. `min_size` stays 3. `max_size` stays 20. Kind and minikube do not apply the HPA. Do not set minDomains (ADR 0107). Catalog stays 221.
  - [x] Single-zone API hostname ceiling — `maxReplicas` is 3, equal to the HPA floor and to `min_size` 3. One Ready zone holds that ceiling at one pod per hostname. The ceiling of 6 is refused. The live set binds on `min_size` 3, so MaxLimitReached at 20 is unreachable. Kind and minikube do not apply the file. Do not set minDomains (ADR 0109). Catalog stays 221.

The Running leader remains the scaler. `priorityClassName` stays `system-cluster-critical`. Kind and minikube do not apply the file. Do not set minDomains (ADR 0110). Catalog stays 221.

Apply refuses a bad chain or a bad SAN before kubectl. Kind and minikube do not apply the file. No `--kubelet-insecure-tls` (ADR 0111). Catalog stays 221.

---

## Phase 5: Long-term Architecture Evolution

**Goal:** Prepare the system for growth and increased complexity.

- Evaluate splitting into bounded contexts (License Service, Provider Gateway, etc.)
- Consider event-driven patterns for revocation and auditing
- Dynamic, admin-managed Pet Catalog
- Multi-tenancy support (if needed)
- Multiplayer (future, dormant): `web/src/lib/multiplayer/p2p.ts` is a WebRTC mesh client that nothing uses. There is no signaling relay (`/api/rtc`) in the tree and no page offers multiplayer. Waking it needs a relay and a reason first; until then no page, menu, or doc should suggest the pets play together online.

It also reads every web and desktop `*-tricks` module and fails when the two disagree, when an idle trick or feed-happy thank-you name is used by two guests (even across the two kinds), or when a guest's soft or hold name is another guest's trick or thank-you. It found seven web rows (fox, hamster, guinea_pig, ferret, hedgehog, turtle, goldfish) still on the pre-#1286 timings while desktop and Python had the Rui-dense 2.4/1.2 set; web now matches. The long hold `hide` sat in four rows: Cup (octopus) keeps it, and Sepia, Chamber and Pulse now hold as `bone`, `spiral` and `bell`, their own hold tricks. - [x] Keeper card server row and plain unlock errors: the overlay no longer shows `Java 8081 · DOWN · unread · unread` to everyone. The row stays hidden until the keeper names a house server (a Backend URL saved in Settings, `COMPUTERPETS_BACKEND_URL`, or the backend a held license came from); then the main process (`desktop/house-server.cjs`) probes that URL's `/api/public/heartbeat` and the card says `House server · reachable` or `House server · unreachable`. Unlock and Signed download turn refused, no-such-host, timeout/abort, `fetch failed`, TLS, and HTTP 5xx/429 failures into one sentence naming the host (`desktop/license/plain-error.cjs`, e.g. "Couldn't reach the house server at <host>. Pets still work without it."); house codes like `no_license` keep their sentences, and the raw error goes to the log, not the window. Harness: `card.house_server`.
- [x] Plain errors on the web desk: the care, hatch, nest and admin pages and the route error screen no longer show raw server or database text (`ECONNREFUSED`, `fetch failed`, `relation "pets" does not exist`, SQLSTATE codes, 5xx/429 bodies). One helper, `web/src/lib/plain-error.ts`, keeps the house's own lines (now thrown as `HouseError`, e.g. "Need 40 ember to hatch a rare companion.") and turns anything else into one plain sentence: can't reach the server, no such host, timed out, secure connection, records not answering, busy, server problem, signed out, bad request, or the page's own fallback. The raw error goes to the server log (`[db] query failed`, `[db] open failed`) or the browser console. `lib/db.ts` converts database failures before they leave the server, with no cause or code attached. The admin page shows the license service's own text only behind a folded "Details" toggle. Test: `web/scripts/plain-error.test.mjs`.
- [x] Short Unlock intro and PowerShell blotter docs: the desktop house window's Unlock section now opens with one line, "Pets work without unlocking. Unlocking is optional." The machine-id privacy note sits folded under **Details**, rewritten as whole sentences that match `desktop/license/hwid.cjs`: the id is read only when `hwid.txt` has no hash, it is mixed with the app name and platform and hashed with SHA-256, only the hash is stored and sent, and the computer-name or random fallback waits for a yes. Backend URL and the Steam fields sit in a box labeled "Optional: only needed to unlock"; the host lines stay visible. `client/README.md` Run has matching bash and PowerShell steps (`py -3 -m venv`, `.\.venv\Scripts\Activate.ps1`, `$env:VAR = ...`) and says the backend URL and license key are optional. `docs/START-HERE.md` has a short "Blotter (optional)" section in PowerShell boxes that start with `cd client`. Tests: `desktop/renderer/settings-intro.test.cjs`, `web/scripts/start-here.test.mjs`.
- [x] Recorded-feed replay in the app harness, and the blotter Unlock detail in whole sentences: the harness now replays saved weather, news, coin, stock, NFT, and GPU responses (`desktop/renderer/fixtures/replay/`, recorded 2026-09-27 and trimmed, no keys) through the real read, parse, and paint with a fake fetch, and checks the words on the plate (`desk.weather.replay`, `desk.news.replay`, `desk.market.replay`, `desk.nft.replay`, `desk.gpu.replay`). `cry.decode` opens every house cry with Python `wave` and checks rate, length, and that it is not silence. App harness 100 → 106 driven. The replays found and fixed two bugs on desktop and web: a Wikipedia featured story reached the news plate as raw markup cut mid-tag, and Google News links over 240 characters were cut. `garter.wav` is all zeros and is listed as a known hole until it is re-exported. The blotter Unlock dialog opens with "Pets work without unlocking. Unlocking is optional." and folds the machine-id detail under a **Details** toggle, in whole sentences checked against `license/hwid.py`; the "If that named read fails." fragment is gone. The blotter weak-fallback yes now reads the computer name on Windows too (it used `os.uname`, which Windows lacks). Painted pixels, drag-and-drop, the Qt blotter scene, and real speakers stay unchecked (docs/APP-HARNESS.md).
- [x] Feed honesty fixes: feed words can no longer become markup on the overlay. The weather, news, coin, NFT, and place-search plates (`desktop/renderer/desk-house.js`, `pet.js`) build elements with `createElement` and text nodes instead of `innerHTML`, and a headline links only an `http(s)` page (`webLink` in `news.js` / `news.ts`; a `javascript:` or relative link stays plain words, on the web desk too). The web desk already rendered feed words as React text. The app harness replays now send `<img src=x onerror=alert(1)>` and `&lt;script&gt;` through every plate and require letters, no element, and no HTML sink; `desktop/renderer/feed-sinks.test.cjs` and `web/scripts/feed-sinks.test.mjs` ban `innerHTML`, `outerHTML`, `insertAdjacentHTML`, and `dangerouslySetInnerHTML` in the overlay renderer and `web/src`. The forecast now says the WMO code in words on desktop and web (`skyWord`: Clear, Mostly clear, Partly cloudy, Overcast, Fog, Drizzle, Rain, Snow, Showers, Thunderstorm, and more; no code reads "Sky unread"), so Seattle's WMO 3 reads Overcast, not Clear; the pets' four art skies are unchanged and no art was added. The Windows counter-only GPU reading now matches Task Manager: processes are added per engine, then the busiest engine of any type is shown, capped at 100 (counter overshoot up to 200 is kept, not refused). The saved counters read 4.2% (video decode), not one process's 0.5% 3D share; desktop, web, and the blotter agree. Tests: `web/scripts/weather-areas.test.mjs`, `news.test.mjs`, `gpu.test.mjs`, `desktop/renderer/gpu.test.cjs`, `client/tests/test_gpu.py`, `test_app_harness.py`; harness rows `desk.*.replay`. `garter.wav` stays silent for now.
- [x] Overlay links open in the browser, and GPU counters are read per adapter: headline, Wikipedia, and favorite links on the overlay now open in the keeper's default browser. The overlay's window-open handler (`desktop/presence/open-link.cjs`, installed by `main.cjs` on both windows) hands only a plain `http(s)` link with a host to `shell.openExternal`, and always answers `deny`, so Electron never makes a window. `javascript:`, `data:`, `file:`, `blob:`, `about:`, `chrome:`, `mailto:`, relative links, links with a name or password, and anything else are refused and logged by scheme only. The overlay still never navigates: `will-navigate`, `will-redirect`, and `will-frame-navigate` stay refused for every URL. Each link carries the line `Opens <host> in your browser` as its title. Windows GPU counters are now grouped by adapter LUID plus physical index, not the index alone, so two adapters that both report `phys_0` no longer mix engines or memory. The counter adapter shown is the one with the most dedicated VRAM (then the most dedicated memory in use, then the most readings), the same on desktop, web, and the blotter. The saved counters still read 4.2%. Tests: `desktop/presence/open-link.test.cjs`, `desktop/renderer/feed-sinks.test.cjs`, `presence.test.cjs`, `gpu.test.cjs`, `web/scripts/gpu.test.mjs`, `client/tests/test_gpu.py`, `test_app_harness.py`; the hand-built `gpu-win-two-adapters.txt` fixture; harness row `desk.links.open` (app harness 106 → 107). Catalog stays 221.
- [x] Garter plays again, and every guest has a cry: `garter.wav` was 4 seconds of zeros, so Sash played nothing. It is re-cut from a CC0 Freesound close recording of wind in dry tall grass (felix.blume #666175), the grass rustle `docs/CRIES.md` promised. Beak (snapping turtle) now plays its own species, an underwater hydrophone grunt from Jorgewich-Cohen et al. 2022 (*Nature Communications*, CC BY 4.0; the video is on the authors' Drive). Jaw (crocodile) plays two juvenile calls of a Nile crocodile, a relative in the same genus, not an American crocodile, from Chabert et al. 2015 (*Scientific Reports*, CC BY 4.0); the synthetic test tones in that file are cut out. Sol (iguana), Shift (chameleon), Spike (horned lizard), and Lid (box turtle) have no open-licensed voice tape, so each plays a CC0 Freesound field recording of its habitat (leaves, bamboo, blown sand, leaf litter) until species tape exists, never a made-up voice. Credits are in `docs/CRIES.md` and `cries-in/SOURCE-*.txt`. Web `prefersHouseCry` also gains Soak (capybara), whose cry was on disk but only wired on desktop. `KNOWN_SILENT_CRIES` is empty and the app harness `cry.decode` reads 221 of 221 cries. Catalog stays 221.
- [x] The start script speaks plainly and finishes the pieces: `desktop.ps1` and `desktop.sh` now check that Node 22 or newer and npm are installed before anything else and stop with one plain sentence if not, instead of a raw `npm is not recognized` error. `node_modules` alone no longer counts as ready: a get-the-pieces run closed halfway (no Electron binary, or no `.computerpets-installed` stamp, which is written only after `npm install` finishes) or a `package.json` newer than the stamp runs `npm install` again, so rerunning the start fixes it as START-HERE promises. A failed `npm install` stops the start in plain words (PowerShell does not stop on a failed native command by itself). `desktop.ps1 -Check` / `sh desktop.sh --check` print the Node version and the pieces state and change nothing; the app harness drives that as **`desk.launch_check`**. Also: `docs/ARCHITECTURE.md` said the Java catalog holds 210 kinds (it holds 221), two `?` stand-ins for dashes in `docs/APP-HARNESS.md` are dashes again, and the web tsc baseline is lowered to 1593. Catalog stays 221.
- [x] The keeper clock keeps time while the overlay is hidden, and the harness drives the main process: the keeper card's alarm and timer used to stop looking whenever the overlay was hidden (Hide the window, a tray click), so an alarm whose minute passed while hidden never rang and a timer rang late. The overlay (`pet.js`) and the desk (`keeper-card.tsx`) now share one `clockTick` that keeps looking while hidden and remembers the last look, so a passed alarm minute (a hidden overlay, a background tab, a sleeping computer) rings once, late, and keeps the day of its moment (23:59 across midnight keeps yesterday); a time already gone when it is set does not ring. A timer rings on the second it ends. A hidden overlay also sends a notification. The weather sound still waits for a visible window. New harness rows load the real `desktop/main.cjs` under a stand-in Electron (`harness_main.cjs`: no window, tray icon, network, or quit; `app.quit` is counted): **`desk.tray.switch`** (On the desk, Companions, `switch-pet`, Hide the window / Show), **`desk.quit`** (`quit-desk`, tray Quit, pet-menu Quit, Turn off asks twice), **`desk.market.search`** (held without the painted line, fake CoinGecko answer, HTTP 500 is unread), **`card.alarm`**, **`card.timer`**, **`card.saved_lines`** (through `card-set` / `card-get` and `card.json`), **`card.music`** (the house loop is a real wav; `radio-search` held, found, and can't reach against a fake Radio Browser), and **`card.mind`** (`mind-set` seals the key, `mind-get` opens it, no store keeps no key). Also: four old ROADMAP entries get their dashes back, and the README and CRIES say which guests play their own species, which quiet guests play the sound their life makes, and that Sol, Shift, Spike, and Lid play habitat recordings. Catalog stays 221.
- [x] A clock notification opens the overlay and its line, and Lula hisses for real: clicking the notification a hidden overlay sends when an alarm or timer rings used to show the overlay and then open the keeper card's care list with no need, so nothing useful showed. Now the click shows the overlay, switches to the guest the clock belongs to, and puts the alarm's or timer's saved line in the speech bubble for six seconds (a say line; a do line, or no line, shows just the overlay). It opens no care. Other notifications still open care. `card.js` builds both the notification and the click command (`clockNote`, `noteCommand`), so `main.cjs` and the overlay share one shape, and the harness rows **`card.alarm`** and **`card.timer`** now click the stand-in notification and read `clock-note` and the line. Lula (boa) played a Freesound "snake hiss" that its maker says was made with a vape. She now plays a real boa hissing a warning, field-recorded in Costa Rica in 1994 by Kjell Persson (Freesound 33458, CC BY 3.0; filed as *Boa constrictor*, a Central American boa that is now often split as *Boa imperator*, same genus). The other snakes were searched on Wikimedia Commons, Freesound, iNaturalist (CC0 and CC BY only), Zenodo, figshare, and the NPS sound gallery. No open-licensed hiss of their own species or genus turned up, so Nori, Saffron, Jade, Sash, Coral, Blush, and Atlas keep their quiet rustles, Bandit keeps his kingsnake hiss, and Bluff keeps a pet snake's hiss (species not stated, now said so in CRIES). Catalog stays 221.
- [x] The House window, Unlock offline, and the whole tray menu are driven by the harness, and five real bugs are fixed. **`desk.settings.window`** loads `settings.html` with its own scripts on a stand-in DOM and the real sandboxed `preload.cjs`, so the Minds and Unlock fields, save, Base URL checks, Details fold, and error lines are read as the keeper sees them. **`desk.license.offline`** runs the real `desktop/license` code through main's IPC against fake house-server answers (valid, expired, wrong machine, network down, 500, 403, and more) with test-only keys and a pre-written `hwid.txt`, so nothing touches the network, a real secret, or the OS machine id. **`desk.tray.menu`** checks every tray row, its enabled state, and its click, plus the software, refused, and no-adapter GPU menus. Bugs fixed: (1) Unlock and Signed download showed developer or server text such as "license expired", "hardware binding mismatch", "LICENSE_SECRET_KEY is missing", or whatever the server put in its error field. Now each case gets one plain sentence ending "Pets still work without it.", and the raw text goes only to the log. (2) Opening the window with an expired stored license showed the raw refusal, because `license-status` skipped the plain words. It no longer does. (3) `license.json` kept the download sign-in token in plain text. It is now sealed in the OS secret store like a plugin key. With no store it stays in memory for that run, and a later download says plainly to unlock again. An older plain token is used once and then sealed. (4) Minds said "Saved" when `mind.json` could not be written. It now says "Not saved" and why. (5) Minds saved Base URLs that talk then refused without a word (http, a local address for a cloud plugin, a private address, not a web address, or a name and password in the URL). Now it names the problem and does not save. Also, Unlock... opens the window at Unlock instead of at Minds, and an empty Steam ID says "Fill in the Steam ID and the App ID first." Catalog stays 221.
- [x] The Python blotter's Unlock now keeps the download sign-in the same way the desktop does, and a few web rough edges are fixed. `license.json` no longer holds the download token in plain text: on Windows it is sealed with DPAPI (this Windows user only), and on macOS/Linux it goes to the OS keychain when the optional `keyring` package is installed (`pip install .[secrets]`; nothing requires it). With no secret store the token stays in memory for that run, the Unlock window says so, and a later Signed download says plainly to unlock again before anything is sent. An older plain token is used once and then sealed (or dropped when there is no store). Unlock and download errors in the blotter are now one plain sentence ending "Pets still work without it." (a port of the desktop's plain-error), naming the house server for refused, not found, timeout, certificate, busy, and HTTP 5xx; the raw error goes only to the `computerpets.license` log. Blank Steam fields say "Fill in the Steam ID and the App ID first." and an unexpected failure is no longer called "unreachable". New harness row **`blotter.unlock_offline`** drives the real Python session against the contract double with test-only keys and a fake codec (sealed, memory-only, migrated, plain words). Web: the admin ledger's 401 is one plain line ("did not accept this admin key… check the key and this computer's clock") and a rejected key is no longer kept in the tab to fail again on every reload; a kennel pet page says "Couldn't open your kennel." when loading fails instead of "Token not in this kennel.", and says "This pet is not in your kennel." when it is really missing; the sign-in page with sign-in off now says what still works and links back to the desk. Catalog stays 221.
- [x] A web page whose first load fails now says so instead of pretending, and the admin ledger checks its address. The kennel (`/collection`) says "Couldn't load your kennel." plus a plain reason and a Try again button instead of showing an empty kennel; the hatchery (`/hatch`) says "Couldn't load your ember." instead of showing 0, and the Draw stays off until the ember loads; the signed-in desk home says "Couldn't load your guests for the desk." with Try again instead of silently showing the default guest; and a failed sign-in click on `/login` shows one plain sentence (a blocked pop-up or a sign-in window closed early are named plainly). All four use the new `loadProblem` in `web/src/lib/plain-error.ts`, so raw error text only reaches the console. Admin: opening the ledger is now a signed read of the license list, so a 404 or an answer that is not a license list says "That address answered, but it isn't the ComputerPets license service." and nothing is saved (a 404 used to count as success). The License service field starts from `VITE_LICENSE_API_URL`, then this site's own address, and uses `http://localhost:8081` only when the page itself runs on this computer; it stays editable with a one-line hint. Ledger times read in the viewer's local time, with the exact ISO instant in the tooltip. New harness row **`web.load_problems`** drives the real `loadProblem` and `admin/base.ts` offline. Catalog stays 221.
- [x] The nest, companion-room play, admin search, and Minds test now say what went wrong, and the web deploy config names its license address. A failed first load on `/nest` says "Couldn't load the nest." plus a plain reason with Try again, instead of 0 ember and no guests. When the house can't save a play in the companion room, one quiet line says "The play wasn't saved, so the meters didn't change." plus the reason, with Try again; the room no longer acts as if the play counted. Admin search and revoke now trust only the license service's own answers: a lookup must return a license row, a 404 counts as "no such license" only when the service says so, and the list must be a license list, so any other answer says "That address answered, but it isn't the ComputerPets license service." A Minds page test that fails now says why (the key was rejected, the service is rate limiting, the address or model is wrong, the service had a problem, the Base URL was refused, it timed out, or it couldn't be reached); the raw text goes only to the console. Deploy: new `web/.env.example` documents `VITE_LICENSE_API_URL` (empty placeholder) and `web/README.md` explains setting it where the site is built. It found a real bug: 42 overlay trick files passed `lastKind | null | undefined` (a TypeScript cast turned into a bitwise OR, always 0) to `pickHappy`, so a thank-you could repeat itself right away; fixed. The impossible comparisons were dead code, removed without changing what runs (`applyCommand`'s seek/eat test, the idle-act 'not sleep' test, `tickVisit`'s 'placed and not leaving'); `presence.cjs`'s key-classifier doc sat on the wrong function; window-play's never-used `IGNORE` opt-out stays as a guard; the six duplicate keys in window-play's api were the same functions listed twice, removed. The Unlock details on Settings and the blotter now say `Opening this window did not look at this computer's ID.` … `The ID itself is never sent.` … `The code still works like a fingerprint for this computer, because this computer always makes the same code.`, and with a saved code `A code is already saved in hwid.txt.`; the license gates' own errors (which reach Settings as-is) say `Nothing was sent to <host>. - [x] Every guest on the web desk now survives a broken step, the real pets are mounted in a test, and music no longer restarts a broken dance. The robin, the bird, the called guests, the plants, and the carried ribbon each had their own animation loop that asked for the next frame at the end, so one thrown error stopped that guest where it stood (a robin frozen mid-air, plants stuck mid-lean). Each loop now runs through the shared frame guard: it asks for the next frame first, logs an error once per error and guest (across visits too), and puts that guest somewhere safe: a robin, bird, or called guest that broke leaves the desk (hidden, its loop stopped; the next call works), the plants stand upright, and the ribbon stays where it was, while their loops keep going. `guardedLoop` (web and overlay, same rules) also ends cleanly when a frame says the guest is gone. A new mount test (`web/scripts/desk-mount.test.mjs`) renders the real `LivingPet` and each guest with React into a small DOM of our own (`web/scripts/mount-dom.mjs`, no new packages), makes a function inside each one throw, and checks the screen: the cat is back on an idle frame with its feet on the floor, the robin and bird leave, one broken guest goes while the other keeps walking. Bug found by that test: with music on, a pet whose dance threw was sent to idle and then started the same broken dance again on the very next frame, because music skips the trick wait; now music waits out an eight-second hold after a broken trick, on the web desk and the overlay (`musicMayDance`). - [x] The overlay robin and bird leave the glass when a frame breaks, and the Minds screens use plain words for the AI picker, the model box, and the key box. - [x] The desk-mount flake is fixed at the root, and the Minds, Unlock, and START-HERE words read plainly. Root cause: `guardedLoop` (web `frame-guard.ts` and the overlay `frame-guard.js`) asks for the next frame before it runs the current one, so when a guest frame returned false (the flight ended) one stale animation frame stayed queued. The bird flight length comes from `Math.random`, and the mount test checks "no frame loop keeps running" after each batch of 60 frames, so about one run in 60 failed (`web.guest_loops_mount` 7/8 once). Fix: a stopped loop now cancels the frame it already asked for (all five desk guests pass `cancelAnimationFrame`), and the mount tests seed `Math.random` (`COMPUTERPETS_MOUNT_SEED`). A new test ends robin and bird flights on every frame of a batch under many seeds. No retries were added. "Save house default" is now **Use for all pets**, and the per-animal choice says **Same as all pets**, word for word on the web desk, the overlay, and the Python client. The `/mind` AI cards show only the name, a plain tag, and a plain blurb. The model ids moved into For builders. Unlock Details on the overlay and the blotter is now one short sentence per line, with every fact kept. The START-HERE "Which sound each pet makes" fold lists one pet per line, and the docs README Minds line starts with the plain /mind top. Harness row `web.flake_house_plain`.
- [x] Mac and Linux keepers are told when the pet pictures did not download, and the Unlock fields use plain words. Bug fixed: the overlay pet pictures (`desktop/renderer/sprites`) are stored with Git LFS. A Git without LFS, common on Mac and Linux, copies small text pointers instead of PNGs, so every pet on the glass was invisible and nothing said why. `desktop.sh` and `desktop.ps1` now check a picture: `--check` / `-Check` prints `pictures: ready`, `missing`, or `lfs-pointers`, and the real start stops before `npm install` with plain Git LFS words (install it, `git lfs install`, `git lfs pull`). START-HERE and the README Mac and Linux steps say to get Git LFS first. A desktop test fails with the same words when the pictures are pointers. The Linux run of every suite was checked on a whole copy of the tree: every earlier box failure came from files missing in the box's partial copy (sounds, sprites, `desktop.sh`, the deploy YAML), not from Linux. Unlock now says **Where you own the game**, **Your Steam ID**, and **Steam App ID**, each with one true helper line (only Steam works here; a Steam ID is 17 digits that start with 7656, under Account details; ComputerPets has no Steam page yet), word for word on the overlay and the blotter. The Pet list reads "Rui · Red Panda" on both, not the catalog key, and the blotter's ask says "Could not read this computer's ID". "random id" is "random ID" everywhere. The START-HERE cry list sits under small room headings (Birds, House pets, Snakes, and so on) with every pet and file kept. Harness row `web.unlock_plain_lfs`.
- [x] The overlay and the web dev server say so when the pet pictures did not download, even without the start scripts. Bug fixed: `cd desktop; npm install; npm start` (the START-HERE fallback when Windows will not run scripts, and the Mac and Linux second way) skipped the Git LFS check, so a Git without LFS still opened a glass of invisible pets. The overlay now reads the same picture as `desktop.sh` and `desktop.ps1` before it opens the glass; pointers or a missing file open a small window with the same Git LFS steps, and the tray keeps them (How to fix, Open git-lfs.com, Quit). The web dev server warns once with the same steps when the site portraits (`web/public/pets`, also Git LFS) are pointers. The Python blotter draws its own pets and loads no picture files, so it has nothing to check. `*.yaml` and `*.yml` are now `eol=lf` in `.gitattributes`: on a Windows checkout (`core.autocrlf=true`) 17 of the 37 deploy checks failed on the carriage returns; now 37/37. The 28 YAML files were already LF in the repo, so only Windows working copies change. One kind name per pet: four bees drifted (Blue Orchard Mason, Alfalfa Leafcutter, Western Honey Bee Drone, Western Honey Bee Queen are the overlay and web roster names); the Python blotter, the web catalog and the backend now use them, and a test holds all 221 names equal across the overlay roster, web roster, web catalog, Python and the backend. START-HERE says the copy is about 4 GB and needs about 8 GB free. Harness rows `desk.pictures_gate` (the real `main.cjs` with pointers opens no glass) and `web.pictures_start_names`.
- [x] Site header on phones: one Menu holds every place, landscape phones reach every care button, and the room rail rests on whole rows. The header scrolled its 27 places sideways at every width: on a phone "Den" was cut in half and "Sign in" wrapped onto two lines, and on a 1280 px laptop "Log" was cut and "Sign in" still wrapped. Now a Menu button holds every place (a disclosure: aria-expanded and aria-controls on the button, a labelled nav of plain links; Escape, a tap outside or tabbing out closes it, and Escape gives focus back to Menu), a laptop shows Meet, Live, Desk, Kennel and Hatchery in the bar, and nothing in the header wraps. On a landscape phone the desk kept the desk window's 520 px floor, so the last row of care buttons sat below the screen where the shell would not scroll to them; the room now fits the phone, the care buttons take the width (two rows), and the left panel starts below the header instead of under it. On a phone the room rail's labels are one row each and snap to the top, and the rail's height is a whole number of rows, so no label rests cut in half (the open drawer no longer scrolls inside the rail). The real-browser sweep (`web/scripts/phone-desk-layout.test.mjs`) now also fails on a clipped, wrapped or off-screen header item, a Menu that does not open, close on Escape, give focus back, or close on a tap outside or tabbing out, a care button off the screen, and a rail label cut at rest (after a real wheel too); and it checks /meet, /catalog, /collection, /login, /hatch, /log and a pet page at a phone and a laptop size. New-keeper audit fixes: the kennel, the hatchery, the nest and a pet page threw a React hydration mismatch on every hard load (the session now reads as loading until hydration, as the server renders it); `npm run dev` printed 22 "will not be code-split" warnings (route files exported their page components); the sign-in tab had no title. Harness row `web.site_header_rail`.
- [x] Small phones: the hello, the species plaque, the room rail and the care buttons never overlap, and speech bubbles draw on top. On a 375×667 iPhone SE and a 360×640 Android the folded plaque still ran under the care buttons; on a 320×568 phone the hello ran under them too and its Got it could not be tapped; at every phone size the room rail (the House list and its open drawer) ran down over the care buttons, the left panel ran under the rail below 390 px wide, and the pet's speech bubble (its own lines and the called guests') drew under the panels. Now the left panel and the rail are measured against the care buttons and end a small gap above them, scrolling inside past that; the rail has a fixed width and the panel stops before it; when even the folded plaque does not fit it shows as one line (Species plaque · About the …) that opens to the whole card; the speech bubble paints above the panels. A real-browser test (`web/scripts/phone-desk-layout.test.mjs`: the system Chrome or Edge, the Vite dev server in-process) sweeps 320, 360, 375, 390 and 414 wide, short and tall, and two landscape phones, and fails on any overlap, a covered Got it, or a bubble under a panel or over the care buttons. A called guest now tells its line once per visit on the web desk and the overlay: a new approach to the pet no longer clears its told mark (the line picker stays as a backstop). The real-window GUI harness removes its own temp folder at the end of a passing run (Chromium holds it until exit, so a small detached helper removes it right after) and keeps it on failure, saying so in its log. The real OS pointer through the overlay's click-through layer is not driven (Electron has no API for OS input; a native input module would change the desktop lockfile; synthetic OS clicks would land on the keeper's real desktop), so `gui.clickthrough_hits` checks the click-through decision in the real window from the hit rects the renderer really sent. `npm run dev` no longer rewrites `web/src/routeTree.gen.ts` (it is checked in the way the generator writes it), so a new contributor's checkout stays clean. Harness rows `web.phone_layout_told_once` and `gui.clickthrough_hits`.
- [x] No close repeats: one line picker for web, overlay and blotter. Pet lines used to be drawn at random with no memory, so the same line came back within seconds, and a called guest re-told its line every time it walked back to the pet (Dee at 0.1 s and again at 7.6 s; the cat's "I sat. You were already here." the same way), because each new approach cleared its told mark. One picker (`web/src/lib/pets/line-picker.ts`, `desktop/renderer/line-picker.js`, `client/computerpets_client/line_picker.py`) now remembers each speaker's last lines: it will not use one of the last 3, nor one said in the last 60 s, unless the pool is too small to avoid it; a guest's one-off line (a tell, the robin's song) stays quiet for 60 s after it was said. Web, overlay and Python pick the same lines from the same seeded rolls; no line's words changed (Rui's stay word for word). The signed-in keeper path is now proved on the mounted `/mind` page with a stand-in house key and a stand-in AI website (no real key): with nothing picked, xAI Grok is In use and Test this mind and desk talk answer from it; an OpenAI pick is In use and answers from OpenAI; without the key, or as a guest, House lines answer and nothing is called. The real overlay window check (`--gui`) now opens the talk bubble and clicks it closed with a real mouse down and up (`gui.bubble_click`). On a phone the first hello and the species plaque ran under the care buttons; now the hello comes first and the plaque waits for Got it, then sits folded (name and kind, About the … to open) above them. The real-window check also stopped leaving a temp folder behind each run. Harness rows `web.no_repeat_signed_in` and `gui.bubble_click`.

- [x] A pasted secret in the model field is dropped before store, post, and fetch. `sk-…`, `key=`, `api_key=`, query-like junk, and a long token count. A normal model id stays. The listener read has no model field. Catalog stays 221. Not DX12.
- [ ] Desktop presence is not filesystem theft: no silent read/write/exfil, no keyloggers, no secret capture. A dropped file is not opened or read. Capture permissions stay denied. Presence writes stay in house files. The desk does not list Desktop, Documents, Downloads, or any other host folder. Window glass stays rects: the enum pipe carries a shell bit, not a class name, title, or path. The enumerator does not call GetClassName. The taskbar and the desktop host are known shell handles. A keeper window's class is not read. A path is omitted unless the keeper has already consented, and there is no such control. A focused card field keeps its typing. A key outside that field is not logged. Escape may dismiss a menu and the key text is not stored. There is no global keyboard hook. A license mark hashes Linux machine-id, Windows MachineGuid, or the Mac platform UUID when Unlock or a bound download has no stored hash. Opening the house window does not read it. The hash stays in hwid.txt and a stored hash is reused, so an existing license stays bound. The raw id is not sent. The hash is still a device fingerprint. Unlock and a bound download name the backend host before that hash leaves. The line says this computer's network address goes with the https request to that host, as any client. A backend on this computer does not send the hash off the machine. Opening the house does not post it. A signed bundle GET names the CDN host before it leaves. The line says this computer's network address goes with the https request to that host, as any client. The license hash is not on that GET. A CDN on this computer, or a local file, does not leave. Opening the house does not fetch it. An unbound download POST names the backend host before it leaves. The line says this download talks to that host, and that this computer's network address goes with the https request, as any client. The license hash is not on that POST. A backend on this computer stays local. Opening the house does not post that download. The unlock hash POST, a bound hash POST, an unbound download POST, and the signed bundle GET leave only inside the refusing wrapper for that host line. A missing or wrong-host line does not post, does not GET, does not read the operating-system id, does not write hwid.txt, and does not say can't reach. A loopback backend or CDN stays on this computer. If that named read fails, Unlock does not mint a computer-name or random mark until the keeper says yes. A computer-name hash changes if the computer is renamed. A random id is used only when there is no computer name, and deleting hwid.txt makes that random id a different mark. The overlay salt stays win32 and the blotter salt stays windows. The overlay plugin key is sealed in the OS secret store when that store exists, and `mind.json` does not keep it in plain text. The desk `/mind` key is not stored in localStorage or sessionStorage. When the desk bridge is up, that key uses the same seal. When the store is missing, the page says so and keeps the key only until the keeper leaves. The overlay Gemini call does not put the plugin key on the query string. A pasted base URL loses `key`, `api_key`, and the same kind of secret query before the direct plugin call, on the overlay and on the house plugin fetch. The overlay model uses the house charset, so a model value cannot add a query to the Gemini path. A pasted secret in that field (`sk-…`, `key=`, `api_key=`, query-like junk, or a long token) is dropped before it is stored or posted, and the fetch uses the preset model. A normal model id stays. The listener read has no model field. Desk talk drops that same secret query from the base URL before it posts. The listener read drops that same query from the saved base URL before it posts. Desk mind prefs drop it on save and on read. `mind.json` and the overlay browser copy of mind prefs drop that same query on save and on read. A leftover dirty base URL in `mind.json` is rewritten without a seal rewrite. The same scrub drops userinfo, a token-shaped path segment, and a `key=` assignment in a string that is not a URL. `/v1/` and a model path stay. The GUI harness may write `COMPUTERPETS_GUI_HARNESS_OUT`; that path is not presence. Geolocation opens only after an in-app yes on a live weather locate, then the session permission closes. Electron cannot revoke a grant Chromium already cached in the renderer (`ResetPermission` is empty). That in-app yes is not a revoke. After the keeper sends the place, or after Don't send, a later `getCurrentPosition` in the same session runs only after a fresh in-app yes. A timer, a forecast refresh, or reopening the weather panel does not call it. A prior browser allow can still satisfy that next locate without a new OS or browser prompt. The house still asks in the app. Electron cannot revoke that cached grant. The page does not read a fix on load, on a timer, from a forecast refresh, or from the first click of the location control. A saved typed area is kept instead of a new locate. A stored live pin is rounded to a tenth of a degree on load. The control says this click sends a place to the forecast host. A live fix is rounded to a tenth of a degree before it leaves. That rounding is not anonymity. The forecast host still sees a place and the client address when a typed city or an acknowledged pin is sent. That later forecast does not leave on load or while the weather panel is closed. Opening the current panel names the send and says this computer's network address goes with the https request, as any client. It does not ask again. There is no forecast timer. Look up of a typed name, and the reverse lookup after Send the place, do not leave until that panel shows this computer's network address goes with the https request to the geocode host, as any client. They do not run on load. A saved live pin does not open a forecast until the keeper says to use that place. That yes sticks for the pin until they clear it or pick another area, and it does not arm geolocation. The weather control does not ask an IP place service when the fix fails. The blotter does not read machine location. News RSS and market quotes do not leave on load or while their plate is closed. Opening the plate shows that this computer's network address goes with the https request, then the send leaves. The twenty-minute refresh sends only while that plate stays open. Radio Find waits until the radio form shows that line for the radio host. A coin or collection look-up waits until the open quotes plate shows that line for the quote host. Pressing Play on a station, or picking one, shows that this computer's network address goes with the https request to that stream host, as any client, and then the audio element opens it. A load does not. A house loop stays on this computer. The blotter does not call those hosts. Pressing Talk on a cloud mind shows that this computer's network address goes with the https request to that talk host, as any client, and then the request leaves. Cloud voice does the same for the voice host. A load does not. House lines, a loopback mind, and on-device speech stay on this computer. A guest stays on house lines. The blotter does not call a talk host or a voice host. News RSS, market quotes, and Radio Find do not leave the main process until the painted line for that host is on the IPC payload. A missing line does not fetch. Find and Local share the radio line. A loopback read stays on this computer. Opening the house does not fetch them. The featured Wikipedia page does not leave until the open news plate shows that this computer's network address goes with the https request to the wikipedia host, as any client. A missing line does not fetch. Opening the house and a closed plate do not fetch it. The desk and `/demo` share that plate. The blotter does not call it. A forecast and a geocode look-up, including the reverse lookup, do not leave until the painted line for that host is present inside the fetch. A missing line does not fetch and does not say can't reach. A closed weather panel does not fetch. The desk and `/demo` share those wrappers. The blotter does not call them. A station stream does not open, and a cloud talk or cloud voice request does not leave, until the painted line for that stream host, talk host, or voice host is present inside the open. A missing line does not assign a stream source, does not call the remote host, and does not say can't reach. A house loop and on-device speech stay on this computer. The desk and `/demo` share those wrappers. The blotter does not call them. The Java heartbeat is a hardcoded fetch of `http://127.0.0.1:8081/api/public/heartbeat` on a timer. No setting points that URL off this computer, so opening the house still polls this house. The desk document does not request a font host. Faces already on this computer are enough. The overlay already uses system faces. Opening the house does not call `fonts.googleapis.com` or `fonts.gstatic.com`.
